    Expanded to 24 pages, the MUFON UFO JOURNAL is unique - the only
    monthly, i l lustrated publication of its kind in the world today wholly devoted to the UFO
    phenomenon in all its myriad mystery. We just celebrated our 20th anni ve rsary and to mark
    the occasion are m aking subscriptions avai lable to the general public at th e rate of $25 a year.


             Each issue contains in-depth, u p- to d ate articles by leading authorities in the UFO
                                                              -




    field. Within the last year alone we have featured articles by Whitley S trieber, Budd
    Hopkins, B arry Greenwood, Gary Kinder, Jenny Randles, Dr. David Jacobs, Hilary Evans
    and Will iam M oore . The present year promises to be equal ly outstanding. Already in the
    works are articles by Linda Moulton Howe (animal mutilations) , Walt Andrus (the amazing
    Florida photograph case), Andy Roberts ("Foo Fighters"), Dr. B ruce Maccabee (an analysis
    of the Alaska satell ite photographs), Peter Rojcewicz ( Men In B lack) , and much m ore.


             In addition, our regu l ar columns feature "The Night Sky," a guide to monthly
    astronom ical phenom ena, "Looking Back," a historical review of UFO activity over the last
    40 years, and "In Others ' Words," a bi-monthly survey of UFO newspaper, magazine and
    book publ i ca ti o ns.


             B ecause we pub l i sh monthly, and thanks to a world-wide network o f field i nvestiga­
    tors, the Journal is able to bring you UFO news almost as soon as it happens, from wherever
    it happens . Our contributors assure you are kept current on the latest theories and
    developments in the field, from analyses of government FOIA docu ments an d UFO
    photographs, to book rev iews, c-om mentary and criticism of the phenomenon that more than
    any o ther has cap tu red the new spac e age imagi nation.
